Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The circulation variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 640 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 866 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 640 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the circulation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 866. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
